<script type="text/javascript">
	jQuery(function($){
		var scrolled = false;
		$(window).scroll(function(){
			    if(140<$(window).scrollTop() && !scrolled){
                    if(!$('.header-container .menu-wrapper .mini-cart').length && !$('.header-container .menu-wrapper .sticky-logo').length){
				    $('.header-container').addClass("sticky-header");
				    var minicart = $('.header-container .mini-cart').html();
				    $('.header-container .menu-wrapper').append('<div class="mini-cart">'+minicart+'</div>');
                                    var logo_image = $('<div>').append($('.header-container .header > .logo').clone()).html();
                    $('.header-container .menu-wrapper').prepend('<div class="sticky-logo">'+logo_image+'</div>');
                                        $(".sticky-logo img").attr("src","https://cdn.daenischesbettenlager.de/media/skin/frontend/dbl/default//images/logo_JYSK-Gans-weiss.png");
                                    				    $('.header-container .header-wrapper > div').each(function(){
					    if($(this).hasClass("container")){
						    $(this).addClass("already");
					    } else {
						    $(this).addClass("container");
					    }
				    });
				    scrolled = true;
                    }
			    }
			    if(140>=$(window).scrollTop() && scrolled){
				    $('.header-container').removeClass("sticky-header");
				    $('.header-container .menu-wrapper .mini-cart').remove();
                                    $('.header-container .menu-wrapper > .sticky-logo').remove();
                				    scrolled = false;
				    $('.header-container .header-wrapper > div').each(function(){
					    if($(this).hasClass("already")){
						    $(this).removeClass("already");
					    } else {
						    $(this).removeClass("container");
					    }
				    });
			    }
		});
	});
</script><script type="text/javascript">

<!-- SEO-Text -->
<hr class="dbl-separator"/>
<div class="dbl-page-text">
<h2>Betten, Möbel, Wohnen – DÄNISCHES BETTENLAGER</h2>
<p>Seit 1984 leben wir unser Motto – Skandinavisch Schlafen &amp; Wohnen. In mittlerweile über 950 Filialen deutschlandweit
		und in unserem Online-Shop bieten wir unseren Kunden alles, was
		<a href="schlafen">Schlafen</a> und
		<a href="haushalt-deko">Wohnen</a> gemütlich macht.
		<a href="moebel" title="Möbel">Möbel</a> wie
		<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/betten" title="Betten">Betten</a>, Boxspringbetten,
		<a href="moebel/wohnmoebel/regale" title="Regale">Regale</a>,
		<a href="moebel/schlafzimmermoebel/kleiderschraenke" title="Kleiderschränke">Kleiderschränke</a>,
		<a href="moebel/esszimmermoebel-kuechenmoebel/esstische" title="Esstische">Esstische</a>,
		<a href="moebel/esszimmermoebel-kuechenmoebel/stuehle" title="Esszimmerstühle">Esszimmerstühle</a>,
		<a href="garten/gartenmoebel" title="Gartenmöbel">Gartenmöbel</a> und 
		<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/matratzen" title="Matratzen">Matratzen</a> für einen erholsamen Schlaf. Auch schöne
		<a href="haushalt-deko/wohnaccessoires/dekoartikel" title="Dekoration">Dekorationen</a> für den Feinschliff können Sie 24 Stunden und 7 Tage die Woche online kaufen. Lassen Sie sich durch unsere
		<a href="moebel" title="Möbel">Möbel</a> Serien inspirieren!</p>
<div class="seo-collapse" id="seoText">
<h3>Online &amp; Offline: Skandinavisch Schlafen &amp; Wohnen</h3>
<p>Unsere Wurzeln sind uns wichtig. Deshalb verkörpern unsere Produkte den skandinavischen Lebensstil und glänzen durch ihr charakteristisches Design. Die wichtigste Maxime dabei lautet: Form folgt Funktion. So sind unsere <a href="moebel" title="Möbel">Möbel</a> nicht nur zeitlos schön, sondern zugleich auch praktisch und nützlich.</p>
<h3>Gut schlafen im perfekten Bett</h3>
<p>Ihr guter Schlaf ist uns wichtig. Deshalb legen wir großen Wert auf individuelle Beratung und nehmen uns die Zeit, gemeinsam
				mit Ihnen das optimale
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme">Schlafsystem</a> für Sie zusammenzustellen. Dieses besteht aus verschiedenen Komponenten, die Sie bei uns erhalten
				und die wir auf Ihre Wünsche abstimmen. Ob
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/lattenroste" title="Lattenrost">Lattenrost</a>,
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/matratzen" title="Matratze">Matratze</a>,
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/bettdecken" title="Bettdecken">Bettdecke</a> oder
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/kopfkissen" title="Kopfkissen">Kopfkissen</a>: Wir bieten Ihnen Qualitätsprodukte namhafter Hersteller wie Schlaraffia, ErgoMAXX® oder Paradies®
				zu sehr günstigen Preisen und eine große Auswahl an modischer und behaglicher
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/bettwaesche" title="Bettwäsche">Bettwäsche</a>. In unseren Filialen und in unserem Online Shop finden Sie die passenden Bettlaken und Bettbezüge
				für Ihr Schlafzimmer. Vertrauen Sie uns Ihren guten Schlaf an und profitieren Sie von unserer langjährigen Erfahrung!</p>
<h3>Gemütlich wohnen in Haus und Garten</h3>
<p>Wir möchten, dass Sie sich jeden Tag auf Ihr Zuhause freuen. Richtig gemütlich wird es mit den richtigen Möbeln und Wohnaccessoires
				für Ihr Esszimmer und Wohnzimmer, Ihr Schlafzimmer, das Kinderzimmer, für Ihre Küche oder Terrasse. Ob klassische
				Massivholzmöbel oder neueste Design-Trends: Geschmackvolle Einrichtung für Ihr Haus und Ihren Garten finden Sie in
				unserem Online-Shop und in unseren Filialen. Auch Dekoration darf nicht fehlen. Bei uns ist eine Vielzahl von Lampen,
				<a href="haushalt-deko/wohnaccessoires/spiegel" title="Spiegel">Spiegel</a>, Kerzen, Decken und Kissen erhältlich, mit denen Sie das richtige Ambiente in Ihrer Wohnung schaffen.</p>
<h3>Ihre Wünsche – schnell und kompetent erfüllt</h3>
<p>Sie freuen sich auf Ihre neuen Möbel und möchten nicht lange warten? Wir möchten das auch nicht. Eigene Lager unserer
				Filialen und eine schnelle Lieferung aus unseren Logistikzentren sorgen deshalb dafür, dass Sie in kürzester Zeit
				gemütlich schlafen und wohnen können. Auch alle Artikel, die Sie online bestellen, sind direkt lieferbar und schnell
				und unkompliziert bei Ihnen. Nutzen Sie die Möglichkeit,
				<a href="schlafen/schlafsysteme/matratzen" title="Matratzen">Matratzen</a> und Möbel vor Ort zu testen – wir sind gerne für Sie da und beraten Sie umfassend in unseren Filialen.
				Kaufen Sie online oder offline - einer unserer Fachmärkte ist bestimmt auch in Ihrer Nähe. Und wenn Sie auf der Suche
				nach Information und Inspiration sind, finden Sie auf unserer
				<a href="https://www.facebook.com/DaenischesBettenlager" target="_blank">Facebook-Seite</a> und bei
				<a href="https://instagram.com/daenisches_bettenlager/" target="_blank">Instagram</a> zahlreiche Anregungen und Neuheiten zu aktuellen Sales und Aktionen.</p>
</div>
<div class="hide-desktop" id="seo-collapse-button"><span class="fa fa-angle-down"></span> Weiterlesen</div>
</div>

<div class="widget widget-static-block"><script>
// Function to swap src with content from data-urlsrc
(function($) {

    var breakpointMobile = 768,
    windowWidth = $(window).width();

    function srcFromDataUrlSrc(selector) {
        $(selector).each(function() {
            var contentUrl = $(this).data('urlsrc');

            // check if content is video
            if($(this).is('video')) {
                $(this).attr('src',contentUrl).attr('poster',contentUrl);
                // check if class js-autoplay ist present to start video
                if($(this).hasClass('js-autoplay')) {
                    $(this).get(0).play();
                }
            } else {
                $(this).attr('src',contentUrl);
            }
        });
    }

    if(windowWidth >= breakpointMobile) {
        srcFromDataUrlSrc('.js-srcFromDataUrlSrc');
    }

})(jQuery);
</script>

    jQuery(window).scroll(function () {
        clearTimeout(windowScroll_t);
        windowScroll_t = setTimeout(function () {
            if (jQuery(this).scrollTop() > 100) {
                jQuery('#totop').fadeIn();
            } else {
                jQuery('#totop').fadeOut();
            }
        }, 500);
    });
    jQuery('#totop').click(function () {
        jQuery('html, body').animate({scrollTop: 0}, 600);
        return false;
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
// -- mobile.js --
(function($){
  $(function() {
    try {
      $('a[href*="catalog/product/view"]').each(function(){
        var path = encodeURIComponent(jQuery(this).parentsUntil("body").map(function(){var x=$(this)[0];return x.tagName+(x.id?'#'+x.id:'')+(x.className?'.'+x.className:'');}).toArray().join('>'));
        var href = $(this).attr('href');
        $.ajax({
          url: 'https://www.daenischesbettenlager.de/dbl_pixel/dummy/pixel?target=' + encodeURIComponent(href) + '&src=' + encodeURIComponent(window.location.href) + '&path=' + path
        });
      });
    } catch (e) {}
  });

<script>

        function mapCheckoutStepToConverlyticsNumber(step) {
            var map = {
                cart: 1,
                login: null,
                billing: 2,
                shipping_method: 2,
                payment: 2,
                review: 3,
                success: 4
            };
            return map[step];
        }

        function mapDeliveryTimeToConverlyticsGroup(deliveryTime) {
            if (deliveryTime <= 2) return 'a';
            if (deliveryTime <= 5) return 'b';
            if (deliveryTime <= 10) return 'c';
            return 'd';
        }

        function mapEcProductToConverlytics(ecProduct, listType, storeAvailability) {
            var mapped = {
                id: ecProduct.id,
                name: ecProduct.name,
                price: ecProduct.price,
                brand: ecProduct.brand,
                category: ecProduct.category,
                dimension10: ecProduct.in_stock ? 'online-available' : 'online-sold-out',
                dimension11: ecProduct.delivery_time,
                dimension12: mapDeliveryTimeToConverlyticsGroup(ecProduct.deliveryTime),
                dimension13: ecProduct.click_and_reserve ? 'store-available' : 'store-sold-out',
                metric2: ecProduct.original_price,
                metric3: ecProduct.net_price
            };

            if (ecProduct.position) {
                mapped.position = ecProduct.position;
            }

            if (storeAvailability) {
                mapped.dimension14 = storeAvailability;
            }

            if (listType) {
                mapped.dimension16 = listType;
            }

            return mapped;
        }

        var currency = 'EUR';

        function sendPageView($, dataLayer) {

            $(function () {
                var data = {
                    'event': 'ga_ec',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': currency
                    }
                };

                var impressionsElements = $('.track-ec-impression');
                if (impressionsElements.length > 0) {
                    data['ecommerce']['impressions'] = [];

                    impressionsElements.each(function (idx, item) {
                        var impressionData = $(item).data() || {};
                        data['ecommerce']['impressions'].push(impressionData.ecProduct);
                    });
                }

                var detailsElements = $('.track-ec-detail');

                if (detailsElements.length > 0) {
                    var detailsEventFired = false;
                    window.addEventListener('Dbl:ClickCollect:Completed', function (ev) {
                        if (detailsEventFired) return;

                        var productViewData = [];
                        productViewData['event'] = 'productView';
                        productViewData['eventCategory'] = 'store';
                        productViewData['ecommerce'] = {};
                        productViewData['ecommerce']['detail'] = {
                            'products': []
                        };

                        var storeData = (ev.detail || {}).stores || [];

                        detailsElements.each(function (idx, item) {
                            var detailData = $(item).data();
                            var product = detailData.ecProduct;

                            var storeAvailability = [];
                            jQuery.each(storeData, function (sidx, sitem) {
                                // On detail page, index 0 should always be the current product
                                storeAvailability.push((sitem.products || [{quantity: 0}]).quantity);
                            });

                            var trackingList = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("productListTracking")) || {};
                            if (trackingList[product.id]) {
                                productViewData['ecommerce']['actionField'] = {list: trackingList[product.id][0]};
                            }

                            productViewData['ecommerce']['detail']['products'].push(mapEcProductToConverlytics(detailData.ecProduct, "", storeAvailability.join("|")));
                        });
                        detailsEventFired = true;
                        dataLayer.push(productViewData);
                    });

                    if (!detailsElements.data('ec-product').click_and_reserve) {
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('Dbl:ClickCollect:Completed'));
                    }


                }

                var promoElements = $('.track-ec-promo');
                if (promoElements.length > 0) {
                    data['ecommerce']['promoView'] = {
                        'promotions': []
                    };

                    promoElements.each(function (index, item) {
                        var itemData = $(item).data();

                        data['ecommerce']['promoView']['promotions'].push({
                            'id': itemData.ecPromoId,
                            'name': itemData.ecPromoName,
                            'creative': itemData.ecPromoCreative || '',
                            'position': itemData.ecPromoPosition || ''
                        });
                    });

                    promoElements.on('click', function () {
                        var itemData = $(this).data();
                        var itemLink = $(this).find('a').attr('href');

                        if (itemData.ecPromoId) {
                            dataLayer.push({
                                'event': 'promotionClick',
                                'ecommerce': {
                                    'promoClick': {
                                        'promotions': [{
                                            'id': itemData.ecPromoId,
                                            'name': itemData.ecPromoName,
                                            'creative': itemData.ecPromoCreative || '',
                                            'position': itemData.ecPromoPosition || ''
                                        }]
                                    }
                                },
                                'eventCallback': function () {
                                    document.location = itemLink;
                                }
                            });
                        }
                    });
                }

                var clickElements = $('.track-ec-click');
                if (clickElements.length > 0) {
                    clickElements.on('click', function () {
                        var clickData = $(this).data();

                        dataLayer.push({
                            'event': 'productClick',
                            'ecommerce': {
                                'click': {
                                    'products': [clickData.ecProduct]
                                }
                            },
                            'eventCallback': function () {
                                document.location = $(this).attr('href');
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }
                dataLayer.push(data);
            });
        }

        sendPageView(jQuery, dataLayer);

        jQuery(function () {
            jQuery(document).on('AJAXCatalog:Result', function () {
                sendPageView(jQuery, dataLayer);
            });
        });


        jQuery(document).on('click', '.track-ec-product-click', function () {
            var clickData = jQuery(this).data();
            if (clickData.ecProduct && clickData.ecUrl) {
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'productClick',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'click': {
                            'products': [clickData.ecProduct]
                        }
                    },
                    'eventCallback': function () {
                        document.location = clickData.ecUrl;
                    }
                })
            }
        });

        jQuery(document).on('click', '.ajax-addtocart', function (ev) {
            var addData = jQuery(this).data();
            if (addData.ecProduct) {
                if (ev.qty && ev.qty > 0) {
                    addData.quantity = ev.qty;
                } else {
                    addData.quantity = 1;
                }
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'addToCart',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': currency,
                        'add': {
                            'actionField':{
                                'action': 'add',
                                'option': 'open-shop'
                            },
                            'products': [addData.ecProduct]
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        });

        jQuery(document).on('click', '.ajax-removefromcart', function (ev) {
            if (ev.isConfirmed && ev.deleteUrl) {
                var removeData = jQuery(this).data();
                if (removeData.ecProduct) {
                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'removeFromCart',
                        'ecommerce': {
                            'remove': {
                                'products': [removeData.ecProduct]
                            }
                        },
                        'eventCallback': function () {
                            document.location = ev.deleteUrl;
                        }
                    });
                    return false;
                }
            }
        });

        function trackRemoveFromCart(products) {
            if (products.length > 0) {
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'removeFromCart',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': currency,
                        'remove': {
                            actionField:{
                                'action': 'removeCartItem',
                                'option': 'open-shop'
                            },
                            'products': products
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        // track removing a single item from cart
        jQuery(document).on('click', '.url-removefromcart', function (ev) {
            if (ev.isConfirmed && ev.deleteUrl) {
                var removeData = jQuery(this).data();
                if (removeData.ecProduct) {
                    trackRemoveFromCart([removeData.ecProduct]);
                }
            }
        });

        // track clear shopping cart
        jQuery(document).on('click', '#empty_cart_button', function (ev) {
            var products = [];
            jQuery(document).find('.url-removefromcart').each(function(idx, item){
                var removeData = jQuery(item).data();
                if (removeData.ecProduct) {
                    products.push(removeData.ecProduct);
                }
            });
            if (products.length > 0) {
                trackRemoveFromCart(products);
            }
        });

        function trackAddToCart(products) {
            if (products.length > 0) {
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'addToCart',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': currency,
                        'add': {
                            'actionField': {
                                'action': 'add',
                                'option': 'open-shop'
                            },
                            'products': products
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        function getProductAddToCartTrackingData(el, type) {
            var converlyticsData = jQuery(el).data().converlyticsProduct;
            if (!converlyticsData) {
                converlyticsData = jQuery(el).data().ecProduct;
            }
            if (converlyticsData) {
                if (type == 'wishlist') {
                    qtySelector = '#wishlist_quantity_' + converlyticsData.id;
                    var qtyElement = jQuery(el.form).find(qtySelector);
                    if (qtyElement.length > 0) {
                        converlyticsData.quantity = qtyElement.val();
                    }
                }

                return converlyticsData;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        }

        // track adding a single product from wishlist to cart
        jQuery(document).on('click', '.btn-addtocart-wishlist-single', function (ev) {
            var productData = getProductAddToCartTrackingData(this, 'wishlist');
            if (productData) {
                trackAddToCart([productData]);
            }
        });

        // track adding all products from wishlist to cart
        jQuery(document).on('click', '.btn-addtocart-wishlist-all', function (ev) {
            var products = [];
            jQuery(this.form).find('.btn-addtocart-wishlist-single').each(function(){
                var productData = getProductAddToCartTrackingData(this, 'wishlist');
                if (productData) {
                    products.push(productData);
                }
            });

            trackAddToCart(products);
        });

        // track reordering products ( from sidebar )
        jQuery(function() {
            jQuery('#reorder-validate-detail').submit(function(){
                var products = [];

                jQuery(this).find('.reorder-checkbox').each(function(){
                    if (this.checked) {
                        products.push(jQuery(this).data().ecProduct);
                    }
                });

                trackAddToCart(products);
            });
        });

        // track adding a product from comparelist to cart
        jQuery(document).on('click', '.compare-list-add-to-cart', function (ev) {
            var productData = getProductAddToCartTrackingData(this);
            if (productData) {
                trackAddToCart([productData]);
            }
        });

        // track updating shopping cart
        jQuery(document).on('click', '.btn-update-shopping-cart', function (ev) {
            var addProducts    = [];
            var removeProducts = [];

            var newCartQuantities = [];
            jQuery(document).find('.validate_cart_quantity').each(function(idx, item){
                var sku = jQuery(item).data('cartItemId');
                var newQty = parseInt(item.value);
                if (sku) {
                    newCartQuantities[sku] = newQty;
                }
            });

            jQuery(document).find('.url-removefromcart').each(function(idx, item){
                var productData = jQuery(item).data().ecProduct;
                if (productData) {
                    var oldQty = parseInt(productData.quantity);
                    var newQty = newCartQuantities[productData.id] > 0 ? newCartQuantities[productData.id] : 0;
                    if (newQty > oldQty) {
                        productData.quantity = newQty - oldQty;
                        addProducts.push(productData);
                    } else if (newQty < oldQty) {
                        productData.quantity = oldQty - newQty;
                        removeProducts.push(productData);
                    }
                }
            });

            if (addProducts.length > 0) {
                trackAddToCart(addProducts);
            }
            if (removeProducts.length > 0) {
                trackRemoveFromCart(removeProducts);
            }
        });

        // Track the most recently viewed lists for each product
        jQuery(function () {
            if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
                try {
                    var maxElements = 1000;

                    var productLists = {};
                    jQuery('.track-ec-impression').each(function (idx, item) {
                        var data = jQuery(item).data('ec-product');
                        productLists[data.id] = [data.list, Date.now()];
                    });

                    var existing = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("productListTracking")) || {};
                    jQuery.each(productLists, function (idx, item) {
                        existing[idx] = item;
                    });
                    var keys = Object.keys(existing);
                    if (keys.length > maxElements) {
                        keys.sort(function (a, b) {
                            return existing[b][1] - existing[a][1];
                        });

                        keys = keys.slice(0, maxElements);

                        var remaining = {};
                        jQuery.each(keys, function (idx, item) {
                            remaining[item] = existing[item];
                        });
                        localStorage.setItem("productListTracking", JSON.stringify(existing));
                    } else {
                        localStorage.setItem("productListTracking", JSON.stringify(existing));
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    localStorage.removeItem("productListTracking")
                }
            }
        });

        jQuery(document).on('click', '.addtocart-form-submit', function (ev) {
            var addData = jQuery(this).data();
            if (addData.ecProduct) {
                if (ev.qty && ev.qty > 0) {
                    addData.ecProduct.quantity = ev.qty;
                } else {
                    addData.ecProduct.quantity = 1;
                }
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'addToCart',
                    'ecommerce': {
                        'currencyCode': currency,
                        'add': {
                            'actionField':{
                                'action': 'add',
                                'option': 'open-shop'
                            },
                            'products': [addData.ecProduct]
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        });

        jQuery(function () {
            jQuery('.track-list').each(function (idx, item) {
                var listData = JSON.parse(item.dataset.list) || {};

                var converlyticsEvent = {
                    event: ((listData.filterAttributes || []).length > 0) ? 'ProductListReload' : 'ProductList',
                    eventCategory: 'store',
                    ecommerce: {
                        currencyCode: listData.currencyCode,
                        impressions: []
                    }
                };

                jQuery('.track-converlytics-list-product').each(function (idx, item) {
                    converlyticsEvent['ecommerce']['impressions'].push(mapEcProductToConverlytics(JSON.parse(item.dataset.converlyticsProduct), listData.list));
                });
                dataLayer.push(converlyticsEvent);
            });
        });

        jQuery(function () {
            jQuery('.track-product-promo').each(function (idx, item) {
                var promoData = JSON.parse(item.dataset.promo) || {};

                var converlyticsEvent = {
                    ecommerce: {
                        currencyCode: promoData.currencyCode,
                        promotions: []
                    }
                };

                jQuery('.track-converlytics-promo-product').each(function (idx, item) {

                    var productData = mapEcProductToConverlytics(JSON.parse(item.dataset.converlyticsProduct));
                    productData.dimension17 = promoData.promo;
                    converlyticsEvent['ecommerce']['promotions'].push(productData);

                    jQuery(this).on('click', function() {
                        dataLayer.push({
                            event: 'promotionClick',
                            ecommerce: {
                                promoClick: {
                                    promotions: [
                                        productData
                                    ]
                                }
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
                dataLayer.push(converlyticsEvent);
            });
        });

        jQuery(function () {
            var checkout = false;
            var converlyticsEvent = {
                'event': 'checkout',
                'ecommerce': {
                    'currencyCode': currency,
                    'purchase': {
                        'actionField': [],
                        'products': []
                    }
                }
            };
            jQuery('.track-converlytics-checkout-cart').each(function (idx, item) {
                converlyticsEvent.ecommerce.purchase.actionField = JSON.parse(item.dataset.converlyticsCart);
                checkout = true;
            });
            jQuery('.track-converlytics-checkout-product').each(function (idx, item) {
                var itemData = JSON.parse(item.dataset.converlyticsProduct);
                converlyticsEvent.ecommerce.purchase.products.push(itemData);
                checkout = true;
            });
            if (checkout) {
                dataLayer.push(converlyticsEvent);
            }
        });

        jQuery(document).on('Dbl:Checkout:GotoSection', function (ev, ed) {
            trackConverlyticsCheckoutStep(jQuery('.track-checkout-step')[0], ed.section);
        });

        function trackConverlyticsCheckoutStep(element, section) {
            var stepData = JSON.parse(element.dataset.trackCheckoutStep) || {};
            var quoteData = JSON.parse(element.dataset.trackCheckoutQuote) || {};

            if (section) {
                stepData.step = section;
            }

            var products = (quoteData.products || []).map(function (item) {
                return mapEcProductToConverlytics(item, null, null);
            });

            var checkoutData = {
                event: 'checkout',
                ecommerce: {
                    currencyCode: quoteData.currencyCode,
                    checkout: {
                        actionField: {
                            step: mapCheckoutStepToConverlyticsNumber(stepData.step),
                            option: stepData.type
                        },
                        products: products
                    }
                }
            };
            dataLayer.push(checkoutData);
        }

        jQuery(function () {
            jQuery('.track-checkout-step').each(function (idx, item) {
                trackConverlyticsCheckoutStep(item, null);
            });
        });

        jQuery(function () {
            (function (jQuery) {
                var sliders = [];
                jQuery('.rev_slider').each(function (idx, item) {
                    var sliderId = item.id;

                    var type = 'slider';

                    if (jQuery(item).hasClass('fullwidthabanner')) {
                        type = 'banner';
                    }

                    jQuery(this).find('.active-revslide:visible').each(function (idx, item) {
                        var slideData = item.dataset || {};
                        sliders.push({
                            id: slideData.ecPromoId,
                            name: slideData.ecPromoName,
                            creative: slideData.ecPromoCreative,
                            position: sliderId,
                            dimension17: type
                        });
                    });

                    if (sliders.length > 0) {
                        dataLayer.push({
                            ecommerce: {
                                promoView: {
                                    promotions: sliders
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }

                    jQuery(this).find('.tp-revslider-slidesli').on('click', function (ev) {
                        var slideData = jQuery(this).data();

                        dataLayer.push({
                            event: 'promotionClick',
                            ecommerce: {
                                promoClick: {
                                    promotions: [
                                        {
                                            id: slideData.ecPromoId,
                                            name: slideData.ecPromoName,
                                            creative: slideData.ecPromoCreative,
                                            position: sliderId,
                                            dimension17: type
                                        }
                                    ]
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);

            if ($nwd_jQuery) {
                (function (jQuery) {
                    var revSlider = jQuery(".rev_slider");
                    revSlider.on("revolution.slide.onloaded", function (e) {
                        var container = jQuery(this);
                        container.find('.tp-leftarrow').on('click', function () {
                            dataLayer.push({
                                'event': 'customEventTracking',
                                'eventCategory': 'slider',
                                'eventAction': 'click',
                                'eventLabel': 'left-arrow'
                            });
                        });

                        container.find('.tp-rightarrow').on('click', function () {
                            dataLayer.push({
                                'event': 'customEventTracking',
                                'eventCategory': 'slider',
                                'eventAction': 'click',
                                'eventLabel': 'right-arrow'
                            });
                        });

                        container.find('.tp-bullet').on('click', function () {
                            dataLayer.push({
                                'event': 'customEventTracking',
                                'eventCategory': 'slider',
                                'eventAction': 'click',
                                'eventLabel': 'navigation-dot'
                            });
                        });
                    });

                    revSlider.on("revolution.slide.onchange", function (e, data) {

                        var sliderId = data.currentslide.context.id || '';
                        var slideData = data.currentslide[0].dataset || {};

                        var type = 'slider';

                        if (jQuery(data.currentslide.context).hasClass('fullwidthabanner')) {
                            type = 'banner';
                        }

                        dataLayer.push({
                            ecommerce: {
                                promoView: {
                                    promotions: [
                                        {
                                            id: slideData.ecPromoId,
                                            name: slideData.ecPromoName,
                                            creative: slideData.ecPromoCreative,
                                            position: sliderId,
                                            dimension17: type
                                        }
                                    ]
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    });
                })($nwd_jQuery);
            }

            jQuery(function() {
                jQuery('.track-filter-add').on('click', function() {
                    var filterData = jQuery(this).data('filter');

                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'customEventTracking',
                        'eventCategory': 'filter',
                        'eventAction': 'list.added',
                        'eventLabel': filterData.attributeCode + '.' + filterData.attributeLabel + '-' + filterData.attributeValue
                    });
                });

                jQuery('.track-filter-remove').on('click', function() {
                    var filterData = jQuery(this).data('filter');

                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'customEventTracking',
                        'eventCategory': 'filter',
                        'eventAction': 'list.remove',
                        'eventLabel': filterData.attributeCode + '.' + filterData.attributeLabel + '-' + filterData.attributeValue
                    });
                });

                jQuery('.track-filter-clear-all').on('click', function() {
                    var filterListData = jQuery(this).data('filter-list');

                    jQuery.each(filterListData, function(idx, item) {
                        dataLayer.push({
                            'event': 'customEventTracking',
                            'eventCategory': 'filter',
                            'eventAction': 'list.remove',
                            'eventLabel': item.attributeCode + '.' + item.attributeLabel + '-' + item.attributeValue
                        });
                    });
                });

                jQuery(document).on('Dbl:Catalog:SliderAdjust', function(ev,ed) {
                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'customEventTracking',
                        'eventCategory': 'filter',
                        'eventAction': 'slider.adjust',
                        'eventLabel': 'price.'+ed.minPrice+'-'+ed.maxPrice
                    });
                })
            });
        });

        function trackSorting(trackData) {
            if (typeof trackData == 'object' && trackData.eventAction) {
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'customEventTracking',
                    'eventCategory': 'sorting',
                    'eventAction': trackData.eventAction,
                    'eventLabel': '/'
                });
            }
        }

        // track changing product list sorting, when using select element
        jQuery(document).on('change', '.track-sorting-select', function (ev) {
            var selectedElement = this.options[this.selectedIndex];
            if (typeof selectedElement == 'object') {
                var trackData = jQuery(selectedElement).data().converlyticsSorting;
                trackSorting(trackData);
            }
        });

        // track changing product list sort direction ( arrow: 'asc' or 'desc' )
        jQuery(document).on('click', '.track-sorting-arrow', function (ev) {
            var trackData = jQuery(this).data().converlyticsSorting;
            if (trackData) {
                trackSorting(trackData);
            }
        });

    </script>
